  383 data QdFpScheme = FastScheme
  384                 | AccurateScheme
  385                 | HighPrecisionScheme
  386   deriving (Show,Eq,Read)
  387 instance Enum QdFpScheme where
  388   succ FastScheme = AccurateScheme
  389   succ AccurateScheme = HighPrecisionScheme
  390   succ HighPrecisionScheme = error "QdFpScheme.succ: HighPrecisionScheme has no successor"
  391 
  392   pred AccurateScheme = FastScheme
  393   pred HighPrecisionScheme = AccurateScheme
  394   pred FastScheme = error "QdFpScheme.pred: FastScheme has no predecessor"
  395 
  396   enumFromTo from to = go from
  397     where
  398       end = fromEnum to
  399       go v = case compare (fromEnum v) end of
  400                  LT -> v : go (succ v)
  401                  EQ -> [v]
  402                  GT -> []
  403 
  404   enumFrom from = enumFromTo from HighPrecisionScheme
  405 
  406   fromEnum FastScheme = 0
  407   fromEnum AccurateScheme = 1
  408   fromEnum HighPrecisionScheme = 2
  409 
  410   toEnum 0 = FastScheme
  411   toEnum 1 = AccurateScheme
  412   toEnum 2 = HighPrecisionScheme
  413   toEnum unmatched = error ("QdFpScheme.toEnum: Cannot match " ++ show unmatched)
  414

  421 data PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType = PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant
  422                                        | PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  423                                        | PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  424   deriving (Show,Eq,Read)
  425 instance Enum PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType where
  426   succ PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant = PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  427   succ PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation = PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  428   succ PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline = error "PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType.succ: PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline has no successor"
  429 
  430   pred PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation = PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant
  431   pred PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline = PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  432   pred PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant = error "PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType.pred: PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant has no predecessor"
  433 
  434   enumFromTo from to = go from
  435     where
  436       end = fromEnum to
  437       go v = case compare (fromEnum v) end of
  438                  LT -> v : go (succ v)
  439                  EQ -> [v]
  440                  GT -> []
  441 
  442   enumFrom from = enumFromTo from PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  443 
  444   fromEnum PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant = 0
  445   fromEnum PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation = 1
  446   fromEnum PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline = 2
  447 
  448   toEnum 0 = PerpetualFuturesPiecewiseConstant
  449   toEnum 1 = PerpetualFuturesLinearInterpolation
  450   toEnum 2 = PerpetualFuturesCubicSpline
  451   toEnum unmatched = error ("PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType.toEnum: Cannot match " ++ show unmatched)

  565 -- |discounts a bond's cash flows off a yield term structure
  566 discountingBondEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
  567  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  568 discountingBondEngine a1 a2 =
  569   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  570   let {a2' = fromMaybeBool a2} in 
  571   preErrorCheck $ \a3' -> 
  572   discountingBondEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' >>= \res ->
  573   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  574   errorCheck  a3'>>
  575   return (res')
  576 
  577 
  578 
  579 -- |Discounts perpetual-futures cashflows to the curves' reference date. The
  580 -- three funding vectors must be non-empty and have identical lengths. The
  581 -- engine supports only 'PerpetualFuturesLinear' and 'PerpetualFuturesInverse'
  582 -- payoffs; QuantLib rejects a Quanto payoff at pricing time.
  583 discountingPerpetualFuturesEngine
  584   :: GenYieldTermStructure y1 -> GenYieldTermStructure y2 -> GenQuote q
  585   -> NonEmpty (Double, Double, Double) -- ^@(fundingTime, fundingRate, interestRateDiff)@
  586   -> PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType -> Double -> IO PricingEngine
  587 discountingPerpetualFuturesEngine domestic foreignCurve spot funding interpolation maxT =
  588   qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine domestic foreignCurve spot times rates diffs interpolation maxT
  589   where (times, rates, diffs) = unzip3 (toList funding)
  590 
  591 qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticDiscountCurve
  592  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignDiscountCurve
  593  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^assetSpot
  594  -> ([Double]) -- ^fundingTimes
  595  -> ([Double]) -- ^fundingRates
  596  -> ([Double]) -- ^interestRateDiffs
  597  -> (PerpetualFuturesInterpolationType) -- ^fundingInterpType
  598  -> (Double) -- ^maxT
  599  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  600 qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
  601   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  602   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  603   withQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  604   withDoubleArray a4 $ \(a4'1, a4'2) -> 
  605   withDoubleArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
  606   withDoubleArray a6 $ \(a6'1, a6'2) -> 
  607   let {a7' = fromEnumC a7} in 
  608   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  609   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
  610   qlDiscountingPerpetualFuturesEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4'1  a4'2 a5'1  a5'2 a6'1  a6'2 a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
  611   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  612   errorCheck  a9'>>
  613   return (res')
  614

  617 -- |discounts a bond's cash flows off a default-risky curve and a flat recovery rate
  618 riskyBondEngine :: (DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -> (Double) -- ^recoveryRate
  619  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  620 riskyBondEngine a1 a2 a3 =
  621   withGenTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  622   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
  623   withYieldTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
  624   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
  625   riskyBondEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
  626   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  627   errorCheck  a4'>>
  628   return (res')
  629

  632 -- |discounts a swap's legs off a single discount curve
  633 discountingSwapEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
  634  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^settlementDate
  635  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^npvDate
  636  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  637 discountingSwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 =
  638   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  639   let {a2' = fromMaybeBool a2} in 
  640   withMaybeDay a3 $ \a3' -> 
  641   withMaybeDay a4 $ \a4' -> 
  642   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
  643   discountingSwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
  644   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  645   errorCheck  a5'>>
  646   return (res')
  647

  650 -- |discounts an FX forward's two legs off their respective currency discount curves
  651 discountingFxForwardEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^sourceCurrencyDiscountCurve
  652  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^targetCurrencyDiscountCurve
  653  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFx
  654  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  655 discountingFxForwardEngine a1 a2 a3 =
  656   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  657   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  658   withQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  659   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
  660   discountingFxForwardEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
  661   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  662   errorCheck  a4'>>
  663   return (res')

  667 -- |Discounts each leg of a 'QuantLib.Instrument.Swap.ConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwap' (or either
  668 -- of its two leaves) off its own currency's discount curve, converting to @domesticCcy@ via
  669 -- @spotFX@ (quoted as units of @domesticCcy@ per unit of @foreignCcy@, w.r.t. a settlement equal
  670 -- to the npv date unless @spotFXSettleDate@ says otherwise). Each leg's stored currency must equal
  671 -- @domesticCcy@ or @foreignCcy@; the two discount curves must share the same reference date.
  672 discountingConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwapEngine :: (Currency) -- ^domesticCcy
  673  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticCcyDiscountCurve
  674  -> (Currency) -- ^foreignCcy
  675  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignCcyDiscountCurve
  676  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFX
  677  -> (Maybe Bool) -- ^includeSettlementDateFlows
  678  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^settlementDate
  679  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^npvDate
  680  -> (Maybe Day) -- ^spotFXSettleDate
  681  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  682 discountingConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
  683   withCurrency a1 $ \a1' -> 
  684   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
  685   withCurrency a3 $ \a3' -> 
  686   withYieldTermStructure a4 $ \a4' -> 
  687   withQuote a5 $ \a5' -> 
  688   let {a6' = fromMaybeBool a6} in 
  689   withMaybeDay a7 $ \a7' -> 
  690   withMaybeDay a8 $ \a8' -> 
  691   withMaybeDay a9 $ \a9' -> 
  692   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
  693   discountingConstNotionalCrossCurrencySwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
  694   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  695   errorCheck  a10'>>
  696   return (res')
  697 
  698 
  699 
  700 -- | CVA/DVA-adjusted swap pricing engine. @invstDTS@\/@invstRecoveryRate@ are the
  701 -- own (investor-side) default probability curve and recovery rate for bilateral
  702 -- CVA\/DVA; pass 'Nothing' for @invstDTS@ and @0.999@ for @invstRecoveryRate@ to
  703 -- match upstream's unilateral-CVA-only defaults.
  704 counterpartyAdjSwapEngine :: (GenYieldTermStructure y) -- ^discountCurve
  705  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^blackVol
  706  -> (DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -- ^ctptyDTS
  707  -> (Double) -- ^ctptyRecoveryRate
  708  -> (Maybe DefaultProbabilityTermStructure) -- ^invstDTS
  709  -> (Double) -- ^invstRecoveryRate
  710  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  711 counterpartyAdjSwapEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
  712   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
  713   withQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
  714   withGenTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
  715   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
  716   withMaybeDefaultProbabilityTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  717   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
  718   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
  719   counterpartyAdjSwapEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
  720   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  721   errorCheck  a7'>>
  722   return (res')
  723

  728 analyticBarrierEngine a1 =

  731   analyticBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' >>= \res ->

  734   return (res')

  738 -- |analytic (Heynen and Kat) pricing engine for a barrier option on two assets, where the first asset's value is compared to the strike and the second's is monitored against the barrier

  747   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
  748   analyticTwoAssetBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
  749   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  750   errorCheck  a4'>>
  751   return (res')

  896 -- |/NB/ Timesteps for Cox-Ross-Rubinstein trees are adjusted using the Boyle-Lau algorithm;
  897 -- pass @maxTimeSteps = timeSteps@ to disable it, or @0@ to use the library's default heuristic.
  898 binomialBarrierEngine :: (BinomialTree) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Word) -- ^timeSteps
  899  -> (Word) -- ^maxTimeSteps
  900  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  901 binomialBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 =
  902   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
  903   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a2 $ \a2' -> 
  904   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
  905   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
  906   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
  907   binomialBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>= \res ->
  908   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  909   errorCheck  a5'>>
  910   return (res')

  914 -- |FX barrier option engine using the vanna-volga method to account for the volatility smile
  915 vannaVolgaBarrierEngine :: (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^atmVol
  916  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Put
  917  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Call
  918  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFX
  919  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticTS
  920  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignTS
  921  -> (Bool) -- ^adaptVanDelta
  922  -> (Double) -- ^bsPriceWithSmile
  923  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  924 vannaVolgaBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
  925   withGenQuote a1 $ \a1' -> 
  926   withGenQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
  927   withGenQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  928   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
  929   withYieldTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  930   withYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
  931   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
  932   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  933   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
  934   vannaVolgaBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
  935   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  936   errorCheck  a9'>>
  937   return (res')
  938

  955 -- |always uses 'AnalyticDoubleBarrierEngine' as the underlying smile-free double-barrier engine
  956 vannaVolgaDoubleBarrierEngine :: (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^atmVol
  957  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Put
  958  -> (DeltaVolQuote) -- ^vol25Call
  959  -> (GenQuote q) -- ^spotFX
  960  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^domesticTS
  961  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^foreignTS
  962  -> (Bool) -- ^adaptVanDelta
  963  -> (Double) -- ^bsPriceWithSmile
  964  -> (Int) -- ^series
  965  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
  966 vannaVolgaDoubleBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
  967   withGenQuote a1 $ \a1' -> 
  968   withGenQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
  969   withGenQuote a3 $ \a3' -> 
  970   withQuote a4 $ \a4' -> 
  971   withYieldTermStructure a5 $ \a5' -> 
  972   withYieldTermStructure a6 $ \a6' -> 
  973   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
  974   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
  975   let {a9' = fromIntegral a9} in 
  976   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
  977   vannaVolgaDoubleBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
  978   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
  979   errorCheck  a10'>>
  980   return (res')

  999 -- |Monte Carlo pricing engine for double-barrier options
 1000 mcDoubleBarrierEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 1001  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 1002  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 1003  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 1004  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 1005  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 1006  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 1007  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 1008  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1009 mcDoubleBarrierEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 1010   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 1011   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 1012   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1013   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 1014   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 1015   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 1016   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 1017   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 1018   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 1019   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 1020   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 1021   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 1022   mcDoubleBarrierEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 1023   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1024   errorCheck  a12'>>
 1025   return (res')

 1191   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 1192   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 1193   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 1194   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 1195   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 1196   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 1197   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 1198   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 1199   mcLookbackPartialFixedEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 1200   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1201   errorCheck  a12'>>
 1202   return (res')

 1206 -- |Monte Carlo pricing engine for continuous partial-time floating-strike lookback options.
 1207 -- Exactly one of @timeSteps@\/@timeStepsPerYear@ must be given; the other must be 'Nothing'.
 1208 mcLookbackPartialFloatingEngine :: (RngTrait) -> (StatisticsTrait) -> (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeSteps
 1209  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^timeStepsPerYear
 1210  -> (Bool) -- ^brownianBridge
 1211  -> (Bool) -- ^antitheticVariate
 1212  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^requiredSamples
 1213  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^requiredTolerance
 1214  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxSamples
 1215  -> (Word) -- ^seed
 1216  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 1217 mcLookbackPartialFloatingEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 1218   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in 
 1219   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 1220   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 1221   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 1222   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 1223   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in 
 1224   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 1225   let {a8' = fromMaybeInt a8} in 
 1226   let {a9' = fromMaybeDouble a9} in 
 1227   let {a10' = fromMaybeInt a10} in 
 1228   let {a11' = fromIntegral a11} in 
 1229   preErrorCheck $ \a12' -> 
 1230   mcLookbackPartialFloatingEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12' >>= \res ->
 1231   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 1232   errorCheck  a12'>>
 1233   return (res')

 1969 -- |The complex-logarithm evaluation formula 'AnalyticHestonEngine' would pick for the given
 1970 -- maturity and Heston parameters when constructed with 'ComplexLogFormula' left to default to
 1971 -- the model's own heuristic (mirrors upstream's asymptotic-characteristic-function threshold).
 1972 analyticHestonEngineOptimalControlVariate :: (Double) -- ^t
 1973  -> (Double) -- ^v0
 1974  -> (Double) -- ^kappa
 1975  -> (Double) -- ^theta
 1976  -> (Double) -- ^sigma
 1977  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 1978  -> (ComplexLogFormula)
 1979 analyticHestonEngineOptimalControlVariate a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 1980   C2HSImp.unsafePerformIO $
 1981   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in 
 1982   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 1983   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 1984   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 1985   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 1986   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 1987   analyticHestonEngineOptimalControlVariate'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 1988   let {res' = (toEnum . fromIntegral) res} in
 1989   return (res')

 2140 -- |American engine based on the QD+ approximation to the exercise boundary. Mainly a good
 2141 -- initial guess for the exercise boundary of 'qdFpAmericanEngine'; usable as a standalone
 2142 -- (lower-accuracy) American pricer on its own.
 2143 qdPlusAmericanEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Word) -- ^interpolationPoints, number of Chebyshev nodes used to interpolate the exercise boundary
 2144  -> (SolverType) -- ^solverType, root-finding method used to locate the exercise boundary
 2145  -> (Double) -- ^eps, solver accuracy
 2146  -> (Maybe Word) -- ^maxIter, solver iteration cap; Nothing uses upstream's default
 2147  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2148 qdPlusAmericanEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
 2149   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2150   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 2151   let {a3' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a3} in 
 2152   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 2153   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 2154   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 2155   qdPlusAmericanEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 2156   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2157   errorCheck  a6'>>
 2158   return (res')
 2159 
 2160 
 2161 
 2162 -- |High performance\/precision American engine based on fixed point iteration for the exercise
 2163 -- boundary (Andersen, Lake and Offengenden 2015; Andersen and Lake 2021). 'QdFpScheme' selects
 2164 -- one of upstream's three built-in 'iterationScheme's ('FastScheme', 'AccurateScheme',
 2165 -- 'HighPrecisionScheme'), trading speed for accuracy.
 2166 qdFpAmericanEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (QdFpScheme) -- ^iterationScheme
 2167  -> (FixedPointEquation) -- ^fpEquation, which fixed-point formulation of the exercise boundary equation to solve
 2168  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2169 qdFpAmericanEngine a1 a2 a3 =
 2170   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2171   let {a2' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a2} in 
 2172   let {a3' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a3} in 
 2173   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
 2174   qdFpAmericanEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
 2175   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2176   errorCheck  a4'>>
 2177   return (res')

 2180 
 2181 -- |Vecer (2001) engine for continuous-averaging arithmetic Asian options, replicating the average
 2182 -- by a self-financing strategy in the underlying and solving the resulting PDE on a finite
 2183 -- @[zMin,zMax]@ grid; requires @zMin <= 0 <= zMax@ and @startDate@ no earlier than the evaluation
 2184 -- date (seasoned Asians are not supported). @currentAverage@ is accepted for parity with upstream's
 2185 -- constructor but is not read by the current implementation (only the not-yet-seasoned case is
 2186 -- handled), so 'Nothing' is fine.
 2187 continuousArithmeticAsianVecerEngine :: (GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess) -> (Maybe (GenQuote q)) -- ^currentAverage
 2188  -> (Day) -- ^startDate
 2189  -> (Word) -- ^timeSteps
 2190  -> (Word) -- ^assetSteps
 2191  -> (Double) -- ^zMin
 2192  -> (Double) -- ^zMax
 2193  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2194 continuousArithmeticAsianVecerEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 =
 2195   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2196   withMaybeQuote a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2197   withDay a3 $ \a3' -> 
 2198   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in 
 2199   let {a5' = fromIntegral a5} in 
 2200   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 2201   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 2202   preErrorCheck $ \a8' -> 
 2203   continuousArithmeticAsianVecerEngine'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' >>= \res ->
 2204   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2205   errorCheck  a8'>>
 2206   return (res')

 2567 -- |Choi (2018) \"sum of Black-Scholes-Merton models\" pricing engine for a basket option on
 2568 -- multiple underlyings, correlated via @rho@
 2569 choiBasketEngine :: NonEmpty GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess -> Matrix Double -- ^correlation matrix rho
 2570   -> Double -- ^lambda, upstream default: 10.0
 2571   -> Word -- ^maxNrIntegrationSteps, upstream default: unbounded; the C shim takes a 32-bit count
 2572   -> Bool -- ^calcfwdDelta
 2573   -> Bool -- ^controlVariate
 2574   -> IO PricingEngine
 2575 choiBasketEngine ps (Matrix mr mc md) = qlChoiBasketEngine (toList ps) mr mc md
 2576 qlChoiBasketEngine :: ([GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess]) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> ([Double]) -> (Double) -- ^lambda
 2577  -> (Word) -- ^maxNrIntegrationSteps
 2578  -> (Bool) -- ^calcfwdDelta
 2579  -> (Bool) -- ^controlVariate
 2580  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2581 qlChoiBasketEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 2582   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcessArray a1 $ \(a1'1, a1'2) -> 
 2583   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 2584   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
 2585   withDoubleArrayRaw a4 $ \a4' -> 
 2586   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 2587   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 2588   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in 
 2589   let {a8' = C2HSImp.fromBool a8} in 
 2590   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 2591   qlChoiBasketEngine'_ a1'1  a1'2 a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 2592   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2593   errorCheck  a9'>>
 2594   return (res')

 2695 -- |Snapshots @rTS@/@fTS@/@fxVolTS@ at construction time (their underlying @shared_ptr@s are copied
 2696 -- out of their handles): a later relink of a 'RelinkableYieldTermStructure' or
 2697 -- 'RelinkableBlackVolTermStructure' passed in here will /not/ be reflected in this 'FdmQuantoHelper'.
 2698 fdmQuantoHelper :: (GenYieldTermStructure y1) -- ^rTS
 2699  -> (GenYieldTermStructure y2) -- ^fTS
 2700  -> (GenBlackVolTermStructure bv) -- ^fxVolTS
 2701  -> (Double) -- ^equityFxCorrelation
 2702  -> (Double) -- ^exchRateATMlevel
 2703  -> IO ((FdmQuantoHelper))
 2704 fdmQuantoHelper a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 =
 2705   withYieldTermStructure a1 $ \a1' -> 
 2706   withYieldTermStructure a2 $ \a2' -> 
 2707   withBlackVolTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
 2708   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 2709   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 2710   preErrorCheck $ \a6' -> 
 2711   fdmQuantoHelper'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' >>= \res ->
 2712   peekFdmQuantoHelper res >>= \res' ->
 2713   errorCheck  a6'>>
 2714   return (res')

 2768 -- |n-dimensional finite-differences Black-Scholes basket-option pricing engine, with an explicit
 2769 -- per-axis grid size
 2770 fdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine :: NonEmpty GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess -> Matrix Double -- ^correlation matrix rho
 2771   -> NonEmpty Word -- ^xGrids, one per underlying
 2772   -> Word -- ^tGrid, upstream default: 50
 2773   -> Word -- ^dampingSteps, upstream default: 0
 2774   -> FdmScheme -- ^schemeDesc, upstream default: 'Douglas'
 2775   -> IO PricingEngine
 2776 fdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine ps (Matrix mr mc md) xGrids = qlFdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine (toList ps) mr mc md (toList xGrids)
 2777 qlFdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine :: ([GeneralizedBlackScholesProcess]) -> (Word) -> (Word) -> ([Double]) -> ([Word]) -> (Word) -- ^tGrid
 2778  -> (Word) -- ^dampingSteps
 2779  -> (FdmScheme) -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 2780 qlFdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 2781   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcessArray a1 $ \(a1'1, a1'2) -> 
 2782   let {a2' = fromIntegral a2} in 
 2783   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
 2784   withDoubleArrayRaw a4 $ \a4' -> 
 2785   withIntArray a5 $ \(a5'1, a5'2) -> 
 2786   let {a6' = fromIntegral a6} in 
 2787   let {a7' = fromIntegral a7} in 
 2788   withFdmSchemeDesc a8 $ \a8' -> 
 2789   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 2790   qlFdndimBlackScholesVanillaEngine'_ a1'1  a1'2 a2' a3' a4' a5'1  a5'2 a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 2791   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 2792   errorCheck  a9'>>
 2793   return (res')

 2985   let {a1' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a1} in

 2987   withGenStochasticProcess a3 $ \a3' -> 
 2988   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 2989   let {a5' = fromMaybeInt a5} in 
 2990   let {a6' = C2HSImp.fromBool a6} in

 3438   let {a3' = fromMaybeInt a3} in 
 3439   let {a4' = fromMaybeInt a4} in 
 3440   let {a5' = C2HSImp.fromBool a5} in

 3576  -> (Word) -- ^timeDependent

 3578  -> (Double) -- ^illegalLocalVolOverwrite
 3579  -> (CashDividendModel) -- ^cashDividendModel
 3580  -> IO ((PricingEngine))
 3581 fdBlackScholesVanillaEngine' a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
 3582   withGeneralizedBlackScholesProcess a1 $ \a1' -> 
 3583   withDividendArray a2 $ \(a2'1, a2'2) -> 
 3584   let {a3' = fromIntegral a3} in 
 3585   let {a4' = fromIntegral a4} in

 3587   withFdmSchemeDesc a6 $ \a6' -> 
 3588   let {a7' = C2HSImp.fromBool a7} in

 3591   preErrorCheck $ \a10' ->

 4041 treeCallableZeroCouponBondEngine' a1 a2 a3 =
 4042   withShortRateModel a1 $ \a1' -> 
 4043   withTimeGrid a2 $ \a2' -> 
 4044   withMaybeYieldTermStructure a3 $ \a3' -> 
 4045   preErrorCheck $ \a4' -> 
 4046   treeCallableZeroCouponBondEngine''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' >>= \res ->
 4047   peekPricingEngine res >>= \res' ->
 4048   errorCheck  a4'>>
 4049   return (res')

 4490   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in

 4782 -- |computes the strike given the option's Black-Scholes delta (in an FX-style delta/vol quotation)
 4783 blackDeltaCalculator :: (OptionType) -> (DeltaType) -> (Double) -- ^spot
 4784  -> (Double) -- ^dDiscount (domestic discount factor)
 4785  -> (Double) -- ^fDiscount (foreign discount factor)
 4786  -> (Double) -- ^stdDev
 4787  -> IO ((BlackDeltaCalculator))
 4788 blackDeltaCalculator a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 =
 4789   let {a1' = fromEnumC a1} in 
 4790   let {a2' = fromEnumC a2} in 
 4791   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 4792   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 4793   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 4794   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 4795   preErrorCheck $ \a7' -> 
 4796   blackDeltaCalculator'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' >>= \res ->
 4797   peekBlackDeltaCalculator res >>= \res' ->
 4798   errorCheck  a7'>>
 4799   return (res')

 5252 -- |Black 1976 implied standard deviation by the Li-Rational-Substitution solver, started from the Radoicic-Stefanica approximation. Pass 'Nothing' for the guess to let QuantLib pick the starting point.
 5253 blackImpliedStdDevLiRS' :: (PlainVanillaPayoff) -> (Double) -- ^forward
 5254  -> (Double) -- ^blackPrice
 5255  -> (Double) -- ^discount
 5256  -> (Double) -- ^displacement
 5257  -> (Maybe Double) -- ^guess
 5258  -> (Double) -- ^omega
 5259  -> (Double) -- ^accuracy
 5260  -> (Word) -- ^maxIterations
 5261  -> IO ((Double))
 5262 blackImpliedStdDevLiRS' a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 =
 5263   withPlainVanillaPayoff a1 $ \a1' -> 
 5264   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 5265   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 5266   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 5267   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 5268   let {a6' = fromMaybeDouble a6} in 
 5269   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 5270   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 5271   let {a9' = fromIntegral a9} in 
 5272   preErrorCheck $ \a10' -> 
 5273   blackImpliedStdDevLiRS''_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' >>= \res ->
 5274   let {res' = realToFrac res} in
 5275   errorCheck  a10'>>
 5276   return (res')

 5630   return (res')

 5636  -> (Double) -- ^forward

 5638  -> (Double) -- ^alpha
 5639  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 5640  -> (Double) -- ^nu
 5641  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 5642  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^volatilityType
 5643  -> IO ((Double))
 5644 unsafeSabrVolatility a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 =
 5645   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in

 5647   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 5648   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 5649   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 5650   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 5651   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 5652   let {a8' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a8} in 
 5653   preErrorCheck $ \a9' -> 
 5654   unsafeSabrVolatility'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' >>= \res ->
 5655   let {res' = realToFrac res} in

 5742 -- |validate SABR parameters, throwing if they are not acceptable
 5743 validateSabrParameters :: (Double) -- ^alpha
 5744  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 5745  -> (Double) -- ^nu
 5746  -> (Double) -- ^rho
 5747  -> IO ()
 5748 validateSabrParameters a1 a2 a3 a4 =
 5749   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in 
 5750   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 5751   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 5752   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 5753   preErrorCheck $ \a5' -> 
 5754   validateSabrParameters'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' >>
 5755   errorCheck  a5'>>
 5756   return ()

 5760 -- |initial guess (alpha, beta, nu, rho) for SABR calibration, per Le Floc'h and Kennedy
 5761 sabrGuess :: (Double) -- ^k_m
 5762  -> (Double) -- ^vol_m
 5763  -> (Double) -- ^k_0
 5764  -> (Double) -- ^vol_0
 5765  -> (Double) -- ^k_p
 5766  -> (Double) -- ^vol_p
 5767  -> (Double) -- ^forward
 5768  -> (Double) -- ^expiryTime
 5769  -> (Double) -- ^beta
 5770  -> (Double) -- ^shift
 5771  -> (VolatilityType) -- ^volatilityType
 5772  -> IO (([Double]))
 5773 sabrGuess a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6 a7 a8 a9 a10 a11 =
 5774   let {a1' = realToFrac a1} in 
 5775   let {a2' = realToFrac a2} in 
 5776   let {a3' = realToFrac a3} in 
 5777   let {a4' = realToFrac a4} in 
 5778   let {a5' = realToFrac a5} in 
 5779   let {a6' = realToFrac a6} in 
 5780   let {a7' = realToFrac a7} in 
 5781   let {a8' = realToFrac a8} in 
 5782   let {a9' = realToFrac a9} in 
 5783   let {a10' = realToFrac a10} in 
 5784   let {a11' = (fromIntegral . fromEnum) a11} in 
 5785   preArray $ \(a12'1, a12'2) -> 
 5786   preErrorCheck $ \a13' -> 
 5787   sabrGuess'_ a1' a2' a3' a4' a5' a6' a7' a8' a9' a10' a11' a12'1  a12'2 a13' >>
 5788   peekDoubleArray  a12'1  a12'2>>= \a12'' -> 
 5789   errorCheck  a13'>>
 5790   return (a12'')
